{\footnotesize\ttfamily static unsigned \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} ata\+\_\+id\+\_\+major\+\_\+version (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const \mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a9e6c91d77e24643b888dbd1a1a590054}{u16}} $\ast$}]{id }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

ata\+\_\+id\+\_\+major\+\_\+version -\/ get A\+TA level of drive @id\+: Identify data

Caveats\+: A\+T\+A-\/1 considers identify optional A\+T\+A-\/2 introduces mandatory identify A\+T\+A-\/3 introduces word 80 and accurate reporting

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} practical impact of this is that ata\+\_\+id\+\_\+major\+\_\+version cannot reliably report on drives below A\+T\+A3. Here is the caller graph for this function\+:
